+void exchangeHalos(std::vector<Real>& input, const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,
+                   const int west, const int east, const int south, const int north,
+                   const int down, const int up, MPI_Datatype xFace, MPI_Datatype yFace,
+                   MPI_Datatype zFace, MPI_Comm comm) {
+    const int sx = nx + 2;
+    const int sy = ny + 2;
+    const size_t origin = (static_cast<size_t>(sy) + 1) * sx + 1;
+    const size_t xWest = origin;
+    const size_t xEast = origin + nx - 1;
+    const size_t ySouth = origin;
+    const size_t yNorth = origin + static_cast<size_t>(ny - 1) * sx;
+    const size_t zDown = origin;
+    const size_t zUp = origin + static_cast<size_t>(nz - 1) * sx * sy;
+    MPI_Request requests[12];
+
+    MPI_Irecv(input.data() + xWest - 1, 1, xFace, west, 10, comm, &requests[0]);
+    MPI_Irecv(input.data() + xEast + 1, 1, xFace, east, 11, comm, &requests[1]);
+    MPI_Isend(input.data() + xWest, 1, xFace, west, 11, comm, &requests[2]);
+    MPI_Isend(input.data() + xEast, 1, xFace, east, 10, comm, &requests[3]);
+    MPI_Irecv(input.data() + ySouth - sx, 1, yFace, south, 20, comm, &requests[4]);
+    MPI_Irecv(input.data() + yNorth + sx, 1, yFace, north, 21, comm, &requests[5]);
+    MPI_Isend(input.data() + ySouth, 1, yFace, south, 21, comm, &requests[6]);
+    MPI_Isend(input.data() + yNorth, 1, yFace, north, 20, comm, &requests[7]);
+    MPI_Irecv(input.data() + zDown - static_cast<size_t>(sx) * sy, 1, zFace, down, 30, comm, &requests[8]);
+    MPI_Irecv(input.data() + zUp + static_cast<size_t>(sx) * sy, 1, zFace, up, 31, comm, &requests[9]);
+    MPI_Isend(input.data() + zDown, 1, zFace, down, 31, comm, &requests[10]);
+    MPI_Isend(input.data() + zUp, 1, zFace, up, 30, comm, &requests[11]);
+    MPI_Waitall(12, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+}
+
+void stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input, std::vector<Real>& output,
+                      const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,
+                      const int x0, const int y0, const int z0,
+                      const int globalNx, const int globalNy, const int globalNz) {
+    const int sx = nx + 2;
+    const int sy = ny + 2;
+    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(sx) * sy;
+    for (int z = 1; z <= nz; ++z) {
+        for (int y = 1; y <= ny; ++y) {
+            for (int x = 1; x <= nx; ++x) {
+                const size_t p = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * sx + x;
+                const bool boundary = x0 + x == 1 || x0 + x == globalNx ||
+                                       y0 + y == 1 || y0 + y == globalNy ||
+                                       z0 + z == 1 || z0 + z == globalNz;
+                if (boundary) {
+                    output[p] = input[p];
+                } else {
+                    output[p] = (input[p] + input[p - 1] + input[p + 1] +
+                                 input[p - sx] + input[p + sx] +
+                                 input[p - plane] + input[p + plane]) / 7.0;
                 }
             }
         }
     }
 }

+struct Range {
+    int begin;
+    int size;
+};
+
+Range splitRange(const int n, const int part, const int parts) {
+    const int remainder = n % parts;
+    return {part * (n / parts) + std::min(part, remainder),
+            n / parts + (part < remainder ? 1 : 0)};
+}
+
+void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,
+                    const int x0, const int y0, const int z0,
+                    const int globalNx, const int globalNy) {
+    const int sx = nx + 2;
+    const int sy = ny + 2;
+    for (int z = 1; z <= nz; ++z) {
+        for (int y = 1; y <= ny; ++y) {
+            for (int x = 1; x <= nx; ++x) {
+                const size_t globalIndex = idx3(static_cast<size_t>(x0 + x - 1),
+                                                static_cast<size_t>(y0 + y - 1),
+                                                static_cast<size_t>(z0 + z - 1),
+                                                static_cast<size_t>(globalNx),
+                                                static_cast<size_t>(globalNy));
+                grid[(static_cast<size_t>(z) * sy + y) * sx + x] =
+                    static_cast<Real>(globalIndex % 19);

         }
     }

+void exchangeHalos(std::vector<Real>& input, const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,
+                   const int west, const int east, const int south, const int north,
+                   const int down, const int up, MPI_Datatype xFace, MPI_Datatype yFace,
+                   MPI_Datatype zFace, MPI_Comm comm) {
+    const int sx = nx + 2;
+    const int sy = ny + 2;
+    const size_t origin = (static_cast<size_t>(sy) + 1) * sx + 1;
+    const size_t xWest = origin;
+    const size_t xEast = origin + nx - 1;
+    const size_t ySouth = origin;
+    const size_t yNorth = origin + static_cast<size_t>(ny - 1) * sx;
+    const size_t zDown = origin;
+    const size_t zUp = origin + static_cast<size_t>(nz - 1) * sx * sy;
+    MPI_Request requests[12];
+
+    MPI_Irecv(input.data() + xWest - 1, 1, xFace, west, 10, comm, &requests[0]);
+    MPI_Irecv(input.data() + xEast + 1, 1, xFace, east, 11, comm, &requests[1]);
+    MPI_Isend(input.data() + xWest, 1, xFace, west, 11, comm, &requests[2]);
+    MPI_Isend(input.data() + xEast, 1, xFace, east, 10, comm, &requests[3]);
+    MPI_Irecv(input.data() + ySouth - sx, 1, yFace, south, 20, comm, &requests[4]);
+    MPI_Irecv(input.data() + yNorth + sx, 1, yFace, north, 21, comm, &requests[5]);
+    MPI_Isend(input.data() + ySouth, 1, yFace, south, 21, comm, &requests[6]);
+    MPI_Isend(input.data() + yNorth, 1, yFace, north, 20, comm, &requests[7]);
+    MPI_Irecv(input.data() + zDown - static_cast<size_t>(sx) * sy, 1, zFace, down, 30, comm, &requests[8]);
+    MPI_Irecv(input.data() + zUp + static_cast<size_t>(sx) * sy, 1, zFace, up, 31, comm, &requests[9]);
+    MPI_Isend(input.data() + zDown, 1, zFace, down, 31, comm, &requests[10]);
+    MPI_Isend(input.data() + zUp, 1, zFace, up, 30, comm, &requests[11]);
+    MPI_Waitall(12, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+}
+
+void stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input, std::vector<Real>& output,
+                      const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,
+                      const int x0, const int y0, const int z0,
+                      const int globalNx, const int globalNy, const int globalNz) {
+    const int sx = nx + 2;
+    const int sy = ny + 2;
+    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(sx) * sy;
+    for (int z = 1; z <= nz; ++z) {
+        for (int y = 1; y <= ny; ++y) {
+            for (int x = 1; x <= nx; ++x) {
+                const size_t p = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * sx + x;
+                const bool boundary = x0 + x == 1 || x0 + x == globalNx ||
+                                       y0 + y == 1 || y0 + y == globalNy ||
+                                       z0 + z == 1 || z0 + z == globalNz;
+                if (boundary) {
+                    output[p] = input[p];
+                } else {
+                    output[p] = (input[p] + input[p - 1] + input[p + 1] +
+                                 input[p - sx] + input[p + sx] +
+                                 input[p - plane] + input[p + plane]) / 7.0;
                 }
             }
         }
     }
 }

+struct Range {
+    int begin;
+    int size;
+};
+
+Range splitRange(const int n, const int part, const int parts) {
+    const int remainder = n % parts;
+    return {part * (n / parts) + std::min(part, remainder),
+            n / parts + (part < remainder ? 1 : 0)};
+}
+
+void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,
+                    const int x0, const int y0, const int z0,
+                    const int globalNx, const int globalNy) {
+    const int sx = nx + 2;
+    const int sy = ny + 2;
+    for (int z = 1; z <= nz; ++z) {
+        for (int y = 1; y <= ny; ++y) {
+            for (int x = 1; x <= nx; ++x) {
+                const size_t globalIndex = idx3(static_cast<size_t>(x0 + x - 1),
+                                                static_cast<size_t>(y0 + y - 1),
+                                                static_cast<size_t>(z0 + z - 1),
+                                                static_cast<size_t>(globalNx),

+void exchangeHalos(std::vector<Real>& input, const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,
+                   const int west, const int east, const int south, const int north,
+                   const int down, const int up, MPI_Datatype xFace, MPI_Datatype yFace,
+                   MPI_Datatype zFace, MPI_Comm comm) {
+    const int sx = nx + 2;
+    const int sy = ny + 2;
+    const size_t origin = (static_cast<size_t>(sy) + 1) * sx + 1;
+    const size_t xWest = origin;
+    const size_t xEast = origin + nx - 1;
+    const size_t ySouth = origin;
+    const size_t yNorth = origin + static_cast<size_t>(ny - 1) * sx;
+    const size_t zDown = origin;
+    const size_t zUp = origin + static_cast<size_t>(nz - 1) * sx * sy;
+    MPI_Request requests[12];
+
+    MPI_Irecv(input.data() + xWest - 1, 1, xFace, west, 10, comm, &requests[0]);
+    MPI_Irecv(input.data() + xEast + 1, 1, xFace, east, 11, comm, &requests[1]);
+    MPI_Isend(input.data() + xWest, 1, xFace, west, 11, comm, &requests[2]);
+    MPI_Isend(input.data() + xEast, 1, xFace, east, 10, comm, &requests[3]);
+    MPI_Irecv(input.data() + ySouth - sx, 1, yFace, south, 20, comm, &requests[4]);
+    MPI_Irecv(input.data() + yNorth + sx, 1, yFace, north, 21, comm, &requests[5]);
+    MPI_Isend(input.data() + ySouth, 1, yFace, south, 21, comm, &requests[6]);
+    MPI_Isend(input.data() + yNorth, 1, yFace, north, 20, comm, &requests[7]);
+    MPI_Irecv(input.data() + zDown - static_cast<size_t>(sx) * sy, 1, zFace, down, 30, comm, &requests[8]);
+    MPI_Irecv(input.data() + zUp + static_cast<size_t>(sx) * sy, 1, zFace, up, 31, comm, &requests[9]);
+    MPI_Isend(input.data() + zDown, 1, zFace, down, 31, comm, &requests[10]);
+    MPI_Isend(input.data() + zUp, 1, zFace, up, 30, comm, &requests[11]);
+    MPI_Waitall(12, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+}
+
+void stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input, std::vector<Real>& output,
+                      const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,
+                      const int x0, const int y0, const int z0,
+                      const int globalNx, const int globalNy, const int globalNz) {
+    const int sx = nx + 2;
+    const int sy = ny + 2;
+    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(sx) * sy;
+    for (int z = 1; z <= nz; ++z) {
+        for (int y = 1; y <= ny; ++y) {
+            for (int x = 1; x <= nx; ++x) {
+                const size_t p = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * sx + x;
+                const bool boundary = x0 + x == 1 || x0 + x == globalNx ||
+                                       y0 + y == 1 || y0 + y == globalNy ||
+                                       z0 + z == 1 || z0 + z == globalNz;
+                if (boundary) {
+                    output[p] = input[p];
+                } else {
+                    output[p] = (input[p] + input[p - 1] + input[p + 1] +
+                                 input[p - sx] + input[p + sx] +
+                                 input[p - plane] + input[p + plane]) / 7.0;
                 }
             }
         }
     }
 }

+struct Range {
+    int begin;
+    int size;

+Range splitRange(const int n, const int part, const int parts) {
+    const int remainder = n % parts;
+    return {part * (n / parts) + std::min(part, remainder),
+            n / parts + (part < remainder ? 1 : 0)};
+}
+
+void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,
+                    const int x0, const int y0, const int z0,
+                    const int globalNx, const int globalNy) {
+    const int sx = nx + 2;
+    const int sy = ny + 2;
+    for (int z = 1; z <= nz; ++z) {
+        for (int y = 1; y <= ny; ++y) {
+            for (int x = 1; x <= nx; ++x) {
+                const size_t globalIndex = idx3(static_cast<size_t>(x0 + x - 1),
+                                                static_cast<size_t>(y0 + y - 1),
+                                                static_cast<size_t>(z0 + z - 1),
+                                                static_cast<size_t>(globalNx),
+                                                static_cast<size_t>(globalNy));

+                    static_cast<Real>(globalIndex % 19);

+void stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input, std::vector<Real>& output,
+                      const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,
+                      const int x0, const int y0, const int z0,
+                      const int globalNx, const int globalNy, const int globalNz) {
+    const int sx = nx + 2;
+    const int sy = ny + 2;
+    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(sx) * sy;
+    for (int z = 1; z <= nz; ++z) {
+        for (int y = 1; y <= ny; ++y) {
+            for (int x = 1; x <= nx; ++x) {
+                const size_t p = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * sx + x;
+                const bool boundary = x0 + x == 1 || x0 + x == globalNx ||
+                                       y0 + y == 1 || y0 + y == globalNy ||
+                                       z0 + z == 1 || z0 + z == globalNz;
+                if (boundary) {
+                    output[p] = input[p];
+                } else {
+                    output[p] = (input[p] + input[p - 1] + input[p + 1] +
+                                 input[p - sx] + input[p + sx] +
+                                 input[p - plane] + input[p + plane]) / 7.0;
                 }
             }
         }
     }

+struct Range {
+    int begin;
+    int size;
+};
+
+Range splitRange(const int n, const int part, const int parts) {
+    const int remainder = n % parts;
+    return {part * (n / parts) + std::min(part, remainder),
+            n / parts + (part < remainder ? 1 : 0)};
+}
+
+void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,
+                    const int x0, const int y0, const int z0,
+                    const int globalNx, const int globalNy) {
+    const int sx = nx + 2;
+    const int sy = ny + 2;
+    for (int z = 1; z <= nz; ++z) {
+        for (int y = 1; y <= ny; ++y) {
+            for (int x = 1; x <= nx; ++x) {
+                const size_t globalIndex = idx3(static_cast<size_t>(x0 + x - 1),
+                                                static_cast<size_t>(y0 + y - 1),
+                                                static_cast<size_t>(z0 + z - 1),
+                                                static_cast<size_t>(globalNx),
+                                                static_cast<size_t>(globalNy));
+                grid[(static_cast<size_t>(z) * sy + y) * sx + x] =
+                    static_cast<Real>(globalIndex % 19);
             }
         }
     }

+void stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input, std::vector<Real>& output,
+                      const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,
+                      const int x0, const int y0, const int z0,
+                      const int globalNx, const int globalNy, const int globalNz) {
+    const int sx = nx + 2;
+    const int sy = ny + 2;
+    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(sx) * sy;
+    for (int z = 1; z <= nz; ++z) {
+        for (int y = 1; y <= ny; ++y) {
+            for (int x = 1; x <= nx; ++x) {
+                const size_t p = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * sx + x;
+                const bool boundary = x0 + x == 1 || x0 + x == globalNx ||
+                                       y0 + y == 1 || y0 + y == globalNy ||
+                                       z0 + z == 1 || z0 + z == globalNz;
+                if (boundary) {
+                    output[p] = input[p];
+                } else {
+                    output[p] = (input[p] + input[p - 1] + input[p + 1] +
+                                 input[p - sx] + input[p + sx] +
+                                 input[p - plane] + input[p + plane]) / 7.0;
                 }
             }
         }
     }
 }

+Range splitRange(const int n, const int part, const int parts) {
+    const int remainder = n % parts;
+    return {part * (n / parts) + std::min(part, remainder),
+            n / parts + (part < remainder ? 1 : 0)};
+}
+
+void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,
+                    const int x0, const int y0, const int z0,
+                    const int globalNx, const int globalNy) {
+    const int sx = nx + 2;
+    const int sy = ny + 2;
+    for (int z = 1; z <= nz; ++z) {
+        for (int y = 1; y <= ny; ++y) {
+            for (int x = 1; x <= nx; ++x) {
+                const size_t globalIndex = idx3(static_cast<size_t>(x0 + x - 1),
+                                                static_cast<size_t>(y0 + y - 1),
+                                                static_cast<size_t>(z0 + z - 1),
+                                                static_cast<size_t>(globalNx),
+                                                static_cast<size_t>(globalNy));
+                grid[(static_cast<size_t>(z) * sy + y) * sx + x] =

+void stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input, std::vector<Real>& output,
+                      const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,
+                      const int x0, const int y0, const int z0,
+                      const int globalNx, const int globalNy, const int globalNz) {
+    const int sx = nx + 2;
+    const int sy = ny + 2;
+    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(sx) * sy;
+    for (int z = 1; z <= nz; ++z) {
+        for (int y = 1; y <= ny; ++y) {
+            for (int x = 1; x <= nx; ++x) {
+                const size_t p = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * sx + x;
+                const bool boundary = x0 + x == 1 || x0 + x == globalNx ||
+                                       y0 + y == 1 || y0 + y == globalNy ||
+                                       z0 + z == 1 || z0 + z == globalNz;
+                if (boundary) {
+                    output[p] = input[p];
+                } else {
+                    output[p] = (input[p] + input[p - 1] + input[p + 1] +
+                                 input[p - sx] + input[p + sx] +
+                                 input[p - plane] + input[p + plane]) / 7.0;
                 }
             }
         }
     }

+Range splitRange(const int n, const int part, const int parts) {
+    const int remainder = n % parts;
+    return {part * (n / parts) + std::min(part, remainder),
+            n / parts + (part < remainder ? 1 : 0)};
+}
+
+void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,
+                    const int x0, const int y0, const int z0,
+                    const int globalNx, const int globalNy) {
+    const int sx = nx + 2;
+    const int sy = ny + 2;
+    for (int z = 1; z <= nz; ++z) {
+        for (int y = 1; y <= ny; ++y) {
+            for (int x = 1; x <= nx; ++x) {
+                const size_t globalIndex = idx3(static_cast<size_t>(x0 + x - 1),
+                                                static_cast<size_t>(y0 + y - 1),
+                                                static_cast<size_t>(z0 + z - 1),
+                                                static_cast<size_t>(globalNx),
+                                                static_cast<size_t>(globalNy));
+                grid[(static_cast<size_t>(z) * sy + y) * sx + x] =
+                    static_cast<Real>(globalIndex % 19);

+void stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input, std::vector<Real>& output,

+            for (int x = 1; x <= nx; ++x) {
+                const size_t p = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * sx + x;
+                const bool boundary = x0 + x == 1 || x0 + x == globalNx ||
+                                       y0 + y == 1 || y0 + y == globalNy ||
+                                       z0 + z == 1 || z0 + z == globalNz;
+                if (boundary) {
+                    output[p] = input[p];
+                } else {
+                    output[p] = (input[p] + input[p - 1] + input[p + 1] +
+                                 input[p - sx] + input[p + sx] +
+                                 input[p - plane] + input[p + plane]) / 7.0;

             }
         }
     }

+Range splitRange(const int n, const int part, const int parts) {
+    const int remainder = n % parts;
+    return {part * (n / parts) + std::min(part, remainder),
+            n / parts + (part < remainder ? 1 : 0)};

+void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,
+                    const int x0, const int y0, const int z0,

+        input[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + x] = recvYS[static_cast<size_t>(z-1) * nx + x-1];
+        input[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + (ny + 1) * sx + x] = recvYN[static_cast<size_t>(z-1) * nx + x-1];
+    }
+    for (int y = 1; y <= ny; ++y) for (int x = 1; x <= nx; ++x) {
+        sendZD[static_cast<size_t>(y-1) * nx + x-1] = input[plane + y * sx + x];
+    }
+    MPI_Sendrecv(sendZD.data(), static_cast<int>(sendZD.size()), MPI_DOUBLE, down, 30,
+                 recvZU.data(), static_cast<int>(recvZU.size()), MPI_DOUBLE, up, 30, comm, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+    for (int y = 1; y <= ny; ++y) for (int x = 1; x <= nx; ++x)
+        sendZU[static_cast<size_t>(y-1) * nx + x-1] = input[static_cast<size_t>(nz) * plane + y * sx + x];
+    MPI_Sendrecv(sendZU.data(), static_cast<int>(sendZU.size()), MPI_DOUBLE, up, 31,
+                 recvZD.data(), static_cast<int>(recvZD.size()), MPI_DOUBLE, down, 31, comm, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+    for (int y = 1; y <= ny; ++y) for (int x = 1; x <= nx; ++x) {
+        input[y * sx + x] = recvZD[static_cast<size_t>(y-1) * nx + x-1];
+        input[static_cast<size_t>(nz + 1) * plane + y * sx + x] = recvZU[static_cast<size_t>(y-1) * nx + x-1];
+    }
+}
+
+void stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input, std::vector<Real>& output,
+                      const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,
+                      const int x0, const int y0, const int z0,
+                      const int globalNx, const int globalNy, const int globalNz) {
+    const int sx = nx + 2;
+    const int sy = ny + 2;
+    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(sx) * sy;
+    for (int z = 1; z <= nz; ++z) {
+        for (int y = 1; y <= ny; ++y) {
+            for (int x = 1; x <= nx; ++x) {
+                const size_t p = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * sx + x;
+                const bool boundary = x0 + x == 1 || x0 + x == globalNx ||
+                                       y0 + y == 1 || y0 + y == globalNy ||
+                                       z0 + z == 1 || z0 + z == globalNz;
+                if (boundary) {
+                    output[p] = input[p];
+                } else {
+                    output[p] = (input[p] + input[p - 1] + input[p + 1] +
+                                 input[p - sx] + input[p + sx] +
+                                 input[p - plane] + input[p + plane]) / 7.0;
                 }
             }
         }
     }
 }

+Range splitRange(const int n, const int part, const int parts) {
+    const int remainder = n % parts;
+    return {part * (n / parts) + std::min(part, remainder),
+            n / parts + (part < remainder ? 1 : 0)};

+void stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input, std::vector<Real>& output,
+                      const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,
+                      const int x0, const int y0, const int z0,
+                      const int globalNx, const int globalNy, const int globalNz) {
+    const int sx = nx + 2;
+    const int sy = ny + 2;
+    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(sx) * sy;
+    for (int z = 1; z <= nz; ++z) {
+        for (int y = 1; y <= ny; ++y) {
+            for (int x = 1; x <= nx; ++x) {
+                const size_t p = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * sx + x;
+                const bool boundary = x0 + x == 1 || x0 + x == globalNx ||
+                                       y0 + y == 1 || y0 + y == globalNy ||
+                                       z0 + z == 1 || z0 + z == globalNz;
+                if (boundary) {
+                    output[p] = input[p];
+                } else {
+                    output[p] = (input[p] + input[p - 1] + input[p + 1] +
+                                 input[p - sx] + input[p + sx] +
+                                 input[p - plane] + input[p + plane]) / 7.0;
                 }
             }
         }
     }
 }

+Range splitRange(const int n, const int part, const int parts) {
+    const int remainder = n % parts;
+    return {part * (n / parts) + std::min(part, remainder),
+            n / parts + (part < remainder ? 1 : 0)};
+}

+void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,
+                    const int x0, const int y0, const int z0,

+
+void stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input, std::vector<Real>& output,
+                      const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,
+                      const int x0, const int y0, const int z0,
+                      const int globalNx, const int globalNy, const int globalNz) {
+    const int sx = nx + 2;
+    const int sy = ny + 2;
+    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(sx) * sy;
+    for (int z = 1; z <= nz; ++z) {
+        for (int y = 1; y <= ny; ++y) {
+            for (int x = 1; x <= nx; ++x) {
+                const size_t p = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * sx + x;
+                const bool boundary = x0 + x == 1 || x0 + x == globalNx ||
+                                       y0 + y == 1 || y0 + y == globalNy ||
+                                       z0 + z == 1 || z0 + z == globalNz;
+                if (boundary) {
+                    output[p] = input[p];
+                } else {
+                    output[p] = (input[p] + input[p - 1] + input[p + 1] +
+                                 input[p - sx] + input[p + sx] +
+                                 input[p - plane] + input[p + plane]) / 7.0;
                 }
             }
         }
     }
 }

+    int begin;
+    int size;

+Range splitRange(const int n, const int part, const int parts) {
+    const int remainder = n % parts;
+    return {part * (n / parts) + std::min(part, remainder),
+            n / parts + (part < remainder ? 1 : 0)};
+}
+
+void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,
+                    const int x0, const int y0, const int z0,
+                    const int globalNx, const int globalNy) {
+    const int sx = nx + 2;
+    const int sy = ny + 2;
+    for (int z = 1; z <= nz; ++z) {
+        for (int y = 1; y <= ny; ++y) {
+            for (int x = 1; x <= nx; ++x) {
+                const size_t globalIndex = idx3(static_cast<size_t>(x0 + x - 1),
+                                                static_cast<size_t>(y0 + y - 1),
+                                                static_cast<size_t>(z0 + z - 1),

+                                                static_cast<size_t>(globalNy));
+                grid[(static_cast<size_t>(z) * sy + y) * sx + x] =

+
+void stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input, std::vector<Real>& output,
+                      const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,
+                      const int x0, const int y0, const int z0,
+                      const int globalNx, const int globalNy, const int globalNz) {
+    const int sx = nx + 2;
+    const int sy = ny + 2;
+    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(sx) * sy;
+    for (int z = 1; z <= nz; ++z) {
+        for (int y = 1; y <= ny; ++y) {
+            for (int x = 1; x <= nx; ++x) {
+                const size_t p = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * sx + x;
+                const bool boundary = x0 + x == 1 || x0 + x == globalNx ||
+                                       y0 + y == 1 || y0 + y == globalNy ||
+                                       z0 + z == 1 || z0 + z == globalNz;
+                if (boundary) {
+                    output[p] = input[p];
+                } else {
+                    output[p] = (input[p] + input[p - 1] + input[p + 1] +
+                                 input[p - sx] + input[p + sx] +
+                                 input[p - plane] + input[p + plane]) / 7.0;
                 }
             }
         }
     }
 }

+struct Range {
+    int begin;
+    int size;
+};
+
+Range splitRange(const int n, const int part, const int parts) {
+    const int remainder = n % parts;
+    return {part * (n / parts) + std::min(part, remainder),
+            n / parts + (part < remainder ? 1 : 0)};
+}
+
+void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,
+                    const int x0, const int y0, const int z0,
+                    const int globalNx, const int globalNy) {
+    const int sx = nx + 2;
+    const int sy = ny + 2;
+    for (int z = 1; z <= nz; ++z) {
+        for (int y = 1; y <= ny; ++y) {
+            for (int x = 1; x <= nx; ++x) {
+                const size_t globalIndex = idx3(static_cast<size_t>(x0 + x - 1),
+                                                static_cast<size_t>(y0 + y - 1),
+                                                static_cast<size_t>(z0 + z - 1),
+                                                static_cast<size_t>(globalNx),
+                                                static_cast<size_t>(globalNy));
+                grid[(static_cast<size_t>(z) * sy + y) * sx + x] =
+                    static_cast<Real>(globalIndex % 19);
             }
         }
     }

+    MPI_Sendrecv(sendXEast.data(), static_cast<int>(sendXEast.size()), MPI_DOUBLE, east, 11,

+    for (int z = 1; z <= nz; ++z) for (int y = 1; y <= ny; ++y) {

+        input[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + y * sx + nx + 1] = recvX[static_cast<size_t>(z-1) * ny + y-1];

+
+void stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input, std::vector<Real>& output,
+                      const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,
+                      const int x0, const int y0, const int z0,
+                      const int globalNx, const int globalNy, const int globalNz) {
+    const int sx = nx + 2;
+    const int sy = ny + 2;
+    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(sx) * sy;
+    for (int z = 1; z <= nz; ++z) {
+        for (int y = 1; y <= ny; ++y) {
+            for (int x = 1; x <= nx; ++x) {
+                const size_t p = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * sx + x;
+                const bool boundary = x0 + x == 1 || x0 + x == globalNx ||
+                                       y0 + y == 1 || y0 + y == globalNy ||
+                                       z0 + z == 1 || z0 + z == globalNz;
+                if (boundary) {
+                    output[p] = input[p];
+                } else {
+                    output[p] = (input[p] + input[p - 1] + input[p + 1] +
+                                 input[p - sx] + input[p + sx] +
+                                 input[p - plane] + input[p + plane]) / 7.0;
                 }
             }
         }
     }
 }

codex
Distributed results now match bit-for-bit across 1, 2, 4, and 8 ranks on test grids, including non-cubic dimensions and multiple iterations. I also removed the temporary datatype path that was producing incorrect halos and retained explicit six-direction exchanges with rank-local buffers, which is robust for arbitrary Cartesian decompositions and `MPI_PROC_NULL` boundaries.
MPI implementation, build configuration, and multi-rank correctness checks are complete.

+
+void stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input, std::vector<Real>& output,
+                      const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,
+                      const int x0, const int y0, const int z0,
+                      const int globalNx, const int globalNy, const int globalNz) {
+    const int sx = nx + 2;
+    const int sy = ny + 2;
+    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(sx) * sy;
+    for (int z = 1; z <= nz; ++z) {
+        for (int y = 1; y <= ny; ++y) {
+            for (int x = 1; x <= nx; ++x) {
+                const size_t p = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * sx + x;
+                const bool boundary = x0 + x == 1 || x0 + x == globalNx ||
+                                       y0 + y == 1 || y0 + y == globalNy ||
+                                       z0 + z == 1 || z0 + z == globalNz;
+                if (boundary) {
+                    output[p] = input[p];
+                } else {
+                    output[p] = (input[p] + input[p - 1] + input[p + 1] +
+                                 input[p - sx] + input[p + sx] +
+                                 input[p - plane] + input[p + plane]) / 7.0;
                 }
             }
         }
     }
 }

codex
Implemented MPI distributed-memory parallelization in:

- [stencil3d.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/stencil3d_gpt-5.6-luna-medium_mpi_r1/stencil3d/stencil3d.cpp)
- [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/stencil3d_gpt-5.6-luna-medium_mpi_r1/stencil3d/CMakeLists.txt)

Highlights:

- 3D Cartesian MPI decomposition via `MPI_Dims_create`
- Six-direction halo exchange per stencil iteration
- Distributed initialization and computation
- Rank-0 result reconstruction preserving existing output semantics
- MPI-aware validation and timing
- Existing executable name preserved: `stencil3d`
- No new files or external dependencies added

Verified successful builds and matching results across 1, 2, 4, and 8 MPI ranks.

+Range splitRange(const int n, const int part, const int parts) {
+    const int remainder = n % parts;
+    return {part * (n / parts) + std::min(part, remainder),
+            n / parts + (part < remainder ? 1 : 0)};
+}
+
+void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,
+                    const int x0, const int y0, const int z0,
+                    const int globalNx, const int globalNy) {
+    const int sx = nx + 2;
+    const int sy = ny + 2;
+    for (int z = 1; z <= nz; ++z) {
+        for (int y = 1; y <= ny; ++y) {
+            for (int x = 1; x <= nx; ++x) {
+                const size_t globalIndex = idx3(static_cast<size_t>(x0 + x - 1),
+                                                static_cast<size_t>(y0 + y - 1),
+                                                static_cast<size_t>(z0 + z - 1),
+                                                static_cast<size_t>(globalNx),
+                                                static_cast<size_t>(globalNy));
+                grid[(static_cast<size_t>(z) * sy + y) * sx + x] =
+                    static_cast<Real>(globalIndex % 19);

         }

+void exchangeHalos(std::vector<Real>& input, const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,

+    std::vector<Real> recvZD(sendZD.size()), recvZU(sendZD.size());
+    for (int z = 1; z <= nz; ++z) for (int y = 1; y <= ny; ++y) {
+        sendX[static_cast<size_t>(z-1) * ny + y-1] = input[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + y * sx + 1];
+        for (int x = 1; x <= nx; ++x) sendYS[static_cast<size_t>(z-1) * nx + x-1] = input[static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + sx + x];
+    }

+    MPI_Sendrecv(sendX.data(), static_cast<int>(sendX.size()), MPI_DOUBLE, west, 10,

+    MPI_Sendrecv(sendXEast.data(), static_cast<int>(sendXEast.size()), MPI_DOUBLE, east, 11,
+                 recvXEast.data(), static_cast<int>(recvXEast.size()), MPI_DOUBLE, west, 11, comm, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+    for (int z = 1; z <= nz; ++z) for (int y = 1; y <= ny; ++y) {

+
+void stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input, std::vector<Real>& output,
+                      const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,
+                      const int x0, const int y0, const int z0,
+                      const int globalNx, const int globalNy, const int globalNz) {
+    const int sx = nx + 2;
+    const int sy = ny + 2;
+    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(sx) * sy;
+    for (int z = 1; z <= nz; ++z) {
+        for (int y = 1; y <= ny; ++y) {
+            for (int x = 1; x <= nx; ++x) {
+                const size_t p = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * sx + x;
+                const bool boundary = x0 + x == 1 || x0 + x == globalNx ||
+                                       y0 + y == 1 || y0 + y == globalNy ||
+                                       z0 + z == 1 || z0 + z == globalNz;
+                if (boundary) {
+                    output[p] = input[p];
+                } else {
+                    output[p] = (input[p] + input[p - 1] + input[p + 1] +
+                                 input[p - sx] + input[p + sx] +
+                                 input[p - plane] + input[p + plane]) / 7.0;
                 }
             }
         }
     }
 }
